## MEH-Z-LOH. 默示錄

### Mz. I.

<u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>應驗個</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>*cong*</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>*Iah-ʽen*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讀個</rb><rt>doh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>*neng-keo*</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫落的</rb><rt>sia-lôh-tih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4, 5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶</rb><rt>*ta*</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>*sing*</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞西亞</rb><rt>Üô-si-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>*læ-tong-go*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>信實</rb><rt>sing-jih</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭一</rb><rt>deo-ih</rt><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>皇帝個</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>滰掉</rb><rt>gyiang-diao</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>*zo*-leh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>個打個</rb><rt>ko-tang-ko</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>戳傷</rb><rt>cʽoh-sông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地裏</rb><rt>di-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>Tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是個</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亞勒巴</rb><rt>Üô-leh-pô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿米加</rb><rt>O-mi-kyüô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>無所不能個</rb><rt>vu-sô-peh-neng-go</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-liao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>按按</rb><rt>en-en</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七粒</rb><rt>tsʽih-lih</rt><rb>星個</rb><rt>sing-go</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>七盞</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsæn</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七粒</rb><rt>tsʽih-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>七</rb><rt>tsʽih</rt><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>七盞</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsæn</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *sia*; Ah-leh-fah teng O-meh-gah, ziu-z Hyi-lah tsʽu-ʽôh li-deo kyʽi-deo teng kyih-sah keh liang-go ing-yüing. 或寫，阿勒法等阿末軋，就是<u>希臘</u>初學裏頭起頭等結煞箇兩個音韻。（原註）</sup>

---

### Mz. II.

「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以弗所</rb><rt>Yi-feh-sô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>順隻</rb><rt>Jing-tsah</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>七粒</rb><rt>tsʽih-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>七盞</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsæn</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走個</rb><rt>tseo-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>勞碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>寬容</rb><rt>kwʽun-yüong</rt><rb>惡人</rb><rt>ôh-nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>實爲</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>弗是個</rb><rt>feh-z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>試試過</rb><rt>s-s-ko</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>假個</rb><rt>kô-go</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>忍氣</rb><rt>jing-kyʽi</rt><rb>耐心</rb><rt>næ-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>疲倦</rb><rt>bi-gyün</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>愛惜個</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>忖到</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tao</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>咋個</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>復轉</rb><rt>voh-cün</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搬過</rb><rt>pun-ko</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一樣</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>尼哥拉</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇黨</rb><rt>keh-tông</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>惡愫個</rb><rt>u-su-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>得勝個</rb><rt>Teh-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>福園</rb><rt>foh-yün</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>8</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>士每拿</rb><rt>Z-me-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>Kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>死仔</rb><rt>si-ts</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>活轉來個</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>難處</rb><rt>næn-cʽü</rt><rb>缺少</rb><rt>kyʽüih-siao</rt></ruby>；（<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsôh</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>猶太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真正</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒但</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-dæn-go</rt><rb>聚會堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>喫個</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-go</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>幾個</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>監裏</rb><rt>kæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>試試</rb><rt>s-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>十日個</rb><rt>jih-nyih-go</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>得勝個</rb><rt>Teh-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>別迦摩</rb><rt>Bih-kyüô-mo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>鋒利</rb><rt>fong-li</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>忠信個</rb><rt>cong-sing-go</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安提帕</rb><rt>En-di-pʽô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>捏定</rb><rt>nyiah-ding</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>*sing*</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一兩樣</rb><rt>ih-liang-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴蘭</rb><rt>Pô-læn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴力</rb><rt>Pô-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>偶像個</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>惡愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥拉</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇黨</rb><rt>keh-tông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>嘴巴裏個</rb><rt>cü-pô-li-go</rt><rb>寶劍</rb><rt>pao-kyin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>得勝個</rb><rt>Teh-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>暗藏個</rb><rt>en-dzông-go</rt><rb>嗎嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一塊</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>白白個</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>簇斬新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-tsæn-sing-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>djü-leh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>識得個</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推雅推喇</rb><rt>Tʽe-yüô-tʽe-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>頂好</rb><rt>ting-hao</rt><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>愛惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>辦事</rb><rt>bæn-z</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>好如</rb><rt>hao-jü</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一兩樣</rb><rt>ih-liang-yiang</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>批評</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶洗別</rb><rt>Yiæ-si-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>候</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教條</rb><rt>kao-diao</rt><rb>誘惑</rb><rt>yiu-ʽôh</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>偶像個</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang-go</rt><rb>祭物</rb><rt>tsi-veh</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>棄掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>回心轉意</rb><rt>we-sing-cün-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>推雅推喇</rb><rt>Tʽe-yüô-tʽe-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>教條</rb><rt>kao-diao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>識得</rb><rt>sih-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所話</rb><rt>sô-wô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>撒但</u>個</rb><rt>Sah-dæn-go</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>擔子</rb><rt>tæn-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>挑</rb><rt>tʽiao</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> ### Mz. III.

「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒狄</rb><rt>Sah-dih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>七粒</rb><rt>tsʽih-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>實惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒狄</rb><rt>Sah-dih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>幾</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>名</rb><rt>ming</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走走</rb><rt>tseo-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>應當個</rb><rt>ing-tông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>得勝個</rb><rt>Teh-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>塗銷</rb><rt>du-siao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>冊子裏</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫信</rb><rt>sia-sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>非拉鐵非</rb><rt>Fi-lah-tʽih-fi</rt><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>聖善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>鑰匙</rb><rt>yiah-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>開仔</rb><rt>kʽæ-ts</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>關仔</rb><rt>kwæn-ts</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>開東</rb><rt>kʽæ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>稍爲</rb><rt>sao-we</rt><rb>有點</rb><rt>yiu-tin</rt><rb>力量</rb><rt>lih-liang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>守住</rb><rt>siu-djü</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招認</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>信</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>老底嘉</rb><rt>Lao-ti-kyüô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>教會個</rb><rt>kyiao-we-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：『<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>造化</rb><rt>zao-hwô</rt><rb>起頭個</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>15</sup> ### Mz. IV.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天裏</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>開間</rb><rt>kʽæ-kæn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>頭遭子</rb><rt>deo-tsao-ts</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>蕩頭</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>團圈</rb><rt>dön-kyʽün</rt><rb>有夥</rb><rt>yiu-ho</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>千千</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>萬萬</rb><rt>væn-væn</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>殺過了個</rb><rt>sah-ko-liao-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>應當</rb><rt>ing-tông</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>富足</rb><rt>fu-tsôh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>力道</rb><rt>lih-dao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地下</rb><rt>di-ʽô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地底下</rb><rt>di-ti-ʽô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海洋裏</rb><rt>hæ-yiang-li</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>萬物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>歸撥</rb><rt>kwe-peh</rt><rb>座位上</rb><rt>zo-we-zông</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>廿四個</rb><rt>nyiæn-s-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. VI.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>開</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>第一顆</rb><rt>di-ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>白</rb><rt>bah</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>騎的個</rb><rt>gyi-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>弓</rb><rt>kong</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第二顆</rb><rt>di-nyi-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>第二個</rb><rt>di-nyi-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>騎的</rb><rt>gyi-tih</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>奪去</rb><rt>deh-kyʽi</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>和平</rb><rt>ʽo-bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>殺來殺去</rb><rt>sah-læ-sah-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第三顆</rb><rt>di-sæn-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>第三個</rb><rt>di-sæn-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>黑</rb><rt>heh</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby></u>；<ruby><rb>騎的個</rb><rt>gyi-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一管</rb><rt>ih-kwun</rt><rb>秤</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>一升</rb><rt>Ih-sing</rt><rb>小麥</rb><rt>siao-mah</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>三升</rb><rt>sæn-sing</rt><rb>大麥</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>賣</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>一鈿</rb><rt>ih-din</rt><rb>銀子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>弄環</rb><rt>long-wæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第四顆</rb><rt>di-s-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>第四個</rb><rt>di-s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一匹</rb><rt>ih-pʽih</rt><rb>灰色個</rb><rt>hwe-seh-go</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>騎的個</rb><rt>gyi-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>Si</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>陰司</rb><rt>Ing-s</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>跟的</rb><rt>keng-tih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>四股得一</rb><rt>s-kwu-teh-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀口</rb><rt>tao-kʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>荒年</rb><rt>hwông-nyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>疫病</rb><rt>yüoh-bing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第五顆</rb><rt>di-ng-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭壇下</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-ʽô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>捏定個</rb><rt>nyiah-ding-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>聖善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>Cü-tsæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>天下</rb><rt>tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>漂白</rb><rt>pʽiao-bah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冤枉</rb><rt>ün-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>幾時</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雪白個</rb><rt>shih-bah-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>告向</rb><rt>kao-hyiang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>且</rb><rt>tsʽia</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>安心</rb><rt>en-sing</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>同事個</rb><rt>dong-z-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>將來</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>滿足</rb><rt>mun-tsôh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第六顆</rb><rt>di-loh-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>震動</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>黑黑個</rb><rt>heh-heh-go</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>毛布</rb><rt>mao-pu</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>變勒</rb><rt>pin-leh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天裏個</rb><rt>tʽin-li-go</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>無花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>大風</rb><rt>do-fong</rt><rb>吹動</rb><rt>cʽü-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>摜落</rb><rt>gwæn-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>熟個</rb><rt>joh-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>Tʽin</rt><rb>過去</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>文書</rb><rt>veng-shü</rt><rb>捲攏</rb><rt>kyün-long</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>山嶺</rb><rt>sæn-ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海島</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>移過</rb><rt>yi-ko</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>世間上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>有佬</rb><rt>yiu-lao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>將軍</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>幽</rb><rt>iu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>山洞</rb><rt>sæn-dong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>山巖裏</rb><rt>sæn-ngæn-li</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>石巖</rb><rt>zah-ngæn</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>蓋住</rb><rt>kæ-djü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>座位上</rb><rt>zo-we-zông</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>；<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶個</rb><rt>ô-wông-go</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>擋勒住</rb><rt>tông-leh-djü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

---

### Mz. VII.

<ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地個</rb><rt>di-go</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>角落頭</rb><rt>kôh-lôh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>四方個</rb><rt>s-fông-go</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>風</rb><rt>fong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吹着</rb><rt>cʽü-djôh</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>旨</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> 「<ruby><rb>嘸得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-ko</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>腦殼頭</rb><rt>nao-kʽôh-deo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>百姓個</rb><rt>pah-sing-go</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>四十四</rb><rt>s-jih-s</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt><rb>打印過了</rb><rt>tang-ing-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>猶大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>流便</rb><rt>Liu-bin</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>迦得</rb><rt>Kyüô-teh</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>亞設</rb><rt>Üô-shih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>納大利</rb><rt>Neh-da-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>瑪拿西</rb><rt>Mô-nô-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西面</rb><rt>Si-min</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以薩迦</rb><rt>Yi-sah-kyüô</rt><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西布倫</rb><rt>Si-pu-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>約瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>便雅憫</rb><rt>Bin-yüô-ming</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>支派</rb><rt>ts-pʽa</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>千</rb><rt>tsʽin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>數</rb><rt>su</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>數弗遍個</rb><rt>su-feh-pin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>雪白個</rb><rt>shih-bah-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>棗樹個</rb><rt>tsao-jü-go</rt><rb>椏枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>攏總</rb><rt>Long-tsong</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>圍轉</rb><rt>yü-cün</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>dæn-nyün</rt><rb>祝讚</rb><rt>coh-tsæn</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>感謝</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉個</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>雪白</rb><rt>shih-bah</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>尊駕</rb><rt>tseng-kô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>曉得個</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>難頭</rb><rt>næn-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>滰勒</rb><rt>gyiang-leh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>雪白個</rb><rt>shih-bah-go</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日日夜夜</rb><rt>nyih-nyih-yia-yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>Sing-din-li</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位上</rb><rt>zo-we-zông</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>肚飢</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>火氣</rb><rt>ho-kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>座位</rb><rt>Zo-we</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>照管</rb><rt>tsiao-kwun</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>活水個</rb><rt>weh-shü-go</rt><rb>源頭</rb><rt>nyün-deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揩掉</rb><rt>kʽa-diao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>眼睛裏</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing-li</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Mz. VIII.

<ruby><rb>開</rb><rt>Kʽæ</rt><rb>第七顆</rb><rt>di-tsʽih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>靜靜個</rb><rt>zing-zing-go</rt><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>半點鐘</rb><rt>pun-tin-cong</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>旁邊</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>香</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>獻上</rb><rt>hyin-zông</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt><rb>上頂</rb><rt>zông-teng</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>香個</rb><rt>Hyiang-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>禱告</rb><rt>tao-kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>馱勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>壇上個</rb><rt>dæn-zông-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𣥼滿仔</rb><rt>tsi-mun-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人聲</rb><rt>nying-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>響雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>馱</rb><rt>Do</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>第一個</rb><rt>Di-ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>三股得一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-teh-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt><rb>攏總</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第二個</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>海水</rb><rt>hæ-shü</rt><rb>三股得一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-teh-ih</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>死掉</rb><rt>si-diao</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>第三個</rb><rt>Di-sæn-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>焰頭</rb><rt>yin-deo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火把</rb><rt>ho-pô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>江河</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水源裏</rb><rt>shü-nyün-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>Keh-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>茵陳</rb><rt>Ing-dzing</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>茵陳</rb><rt>ing-dzing</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第四個</rb><rt>Di-s-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>打掉</rb><rt>tang-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>日裏</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>飛上</rb><rt>fi-zông</rt><rb>半天裏</rb><rt>pun-tʽin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽO-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>！」

### Mz. IX.

<ruby><rb>第五個</rb><rt>Di-ng-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一粒</rb><rt>ih-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>跌落</rb><rt>tih-lôh</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>嘸底坑</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang</rt><rb>鑰匙</rb><rt>yiah-z</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>嘸底坑</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>猛猛</rb><rt>mang-mang</rt><rb>火爐個</rb><rt>ho-lu-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>坑裏</rb><rt>kʽang-li</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>日頭</rb><rt>Nyih-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>穹蒼</rb><rt>kyʽüong-tsʽông</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>坑裏個</rb><rt>kʽang-li-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>黑暗了</rb><rt>heh-en-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>煙裏</rb><rt>in-li</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>蠍子</rb><rt>hyih-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>奉</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>旨</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>草</rb><rt>tsʽao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>碧綠個</rb><rt>pih-loh-go</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>樹木</rb><rt>jü-moh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>額角裏</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-li</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>打的</rb><rt>tang-tih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>Sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>蠍子</rb><rt>hyih-ts</rt><rb>叮</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尋</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尋弗來</rb><rt>zing-feh-læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>巴弗能夠</rb><rt>pô-feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>避開</rb><rt>bi-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>蝗蟲</rb><rt>wông-djong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>女人個</rb><rt>nyü-nying-go</rt><rb>頭髮</rb><rt>deo-fah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>獅子個</rb><rt>s-ts-go</rt><rb>牙齒</rb><rt>ngô-tsʽ</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>護心鏡</rb><rt>wu-sing-kying</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>鐵甲</rb><rt>tʽih-kah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>翼梢個</rb><rt>yih-sao-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>奔</rb><rt>peng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蠍子</rb><rt>hyih-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尾巴裏</rb><rt>mi-pô-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>五個</rb><rt>ng-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>嘸底坑個</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞巴頓</rb><rt>Üô-pô-teng</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希利尼</rb><rt>Hyi-li-nyi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞玻倫</rb><rt>Üô-po-leng</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 zʽeo</sup>

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>第六個</rb><rt>Di-loh-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>金壇個</rb><rt>kying-dæn-go</rt><rb>四角</rb><rt>s-kôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>第六個</rb><rt>di-loh-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>縛</rb><rt>Bo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉</rb><rt>Pah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>定好個</rb><rt>ding-hao-go</rt><rb>時辰</rb><rt>z-zing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>月份</rb><rt>yüih-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>年庚</rb><rt>nyin-kang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>馬兵個</rb><rt>Mô-ping-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>二萬萬</rb><rt>nyi-væn-væn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>逢</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>默示</rb><rt>meh-z</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>騎的個</rb><rt>gyi-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>護心鏡</rb><rt>wu-sing-kying</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>紫紅</rb><rt>ts-ʽong</rt><rb>色</rb><rt>seh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>馬</rb><rt>Mô</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>噴出</rb><rt>pʽeng-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>Yüong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>三樣</rb><rt>sæn-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>噴出個</rb><rt>pʽeng-cʽih-go</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>馬個</rb><rt>Mô-go</rt><rb>勢道</rb><rt>shü-dao</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用勒</rb><rt>yüong-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>Yü-to-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>遭着</rb><rt>tsao-djôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做個</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>木頭</rb><rt>moh-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>石頭</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>行動</rb><rt>ʽang-dong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>兇手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>邪術</rb><rt>zia-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>偷盜</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. X.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>能幹個</rb><rt>neng-ken-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>鱟</rb><rt>heo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火柱</rb><rt>ho-djü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>；<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>馱</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一卷</rb><rt>ih-kyün</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt><rb>書</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>攤開的</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>順隻</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏</rb><rt>dah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海上</rb><rt>hæ-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>借隻</rb><rt>tsia-tsah</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>踏</rb><rt>dah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>；<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳出來</rb><rt>eo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>獅子</rb><rt>s-ts</rt><rb>哮</rb><rt>hyiao</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>謳好仔</rb><rt>Eo-hao-ts</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>發出</rb><rt>fah-cʽih</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>講好仔</rb><rt>kông-hao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>打賬</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>雷</rb><rt>le</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>封好</rb><rt>fong-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>可</rb><rt>kʽo</rt><rb>寫出</rb><rt>sia-cʽih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>提</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>永活個</rb><rt>üong-weh-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>罰咒</rb><rt>vah-tsiu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>時候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>第七個</rb><rt>di-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>成功</rb><rt>dzing-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好好</rb><rt>hao-hao</rt><rb>信息</rb><rt>sing-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>傳</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>來個</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>攤開間個</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ-kæn-go</rt><rb>小卷</rb><rt>siao-kyün</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>請</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小卷</rb><rt>siao-kyün</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>馱去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>肚皮裏</rb><rt>du-bi-li</rt><rb>苦</rb><rt>kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>甜</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>小卷</rb><rt>siao-kyün</rt><rb>喫了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>果然</rb><rt>ko-jün</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>蜜糖</rb><rt>mih-dông</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>甜</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>之後</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>苦個</rb><rt>kwʽu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>君王個</rb><rt>kyüing-wông-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。」
