### Mz. XI.

<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>棍子</rb><rt>kweng-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>丈尺</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽih</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>禮拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>單只</rb><rt>Tæn-tsih</rt><rb>除出</rb><rt>djü-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿外個</rb><rt>sing-din-nga-go</rt><rb>圍牆</rb><rt>yü-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>別國人</rb><rt>bih-koh-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>踔糊</rb><rt>nao-wu</rt><rb>聖城</rb><rt>sing-dzing</rt><rb>四十二個</rb><rt>s-jih-nyi-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>見證</rb><rt>kyin-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>麻衣</rb><rt>mô-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>兩株</rb><rt>liang-cü</rt><rb>橄欖</rb><rt>ken-læn</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>兩管</rb><rt>liang-kwun</rt><rb>燈臺</rb><rt>teng-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>主個</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>噴出</rb><rt>pʽeng-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滅掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺</rb><rt>sah</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>塞住</rb><rt>seh-djü</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>當</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>落雨</rb><rt>lôh-yü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>隨</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>要個</rb><rt>iao-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>打着</rb><rt>tang-djôh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>做滿</rb><rt>tso-mun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸底坑</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang</rt><rb>走上個</rb><rt>tseo-zông-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子個</rb><rt>dzing-ts-go</rt><rb>路街裏</rb><rt>lu-ka-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>比方</rb><rt>pi-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>所多瑪</rb><rt>Sô-to-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>埃及</rb><rt>Yiæ-gyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>釘</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有得個</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>安葬</rb><rt>en-tsông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>屍首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>墳墓裏</rb><rt>veng-mo-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>禮物</rb><rt>li-veh</rt><rb>送來送去</rb><rt>song-læ-song-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兩個</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>磨難</rb><rt>mo-næn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>過了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>半</rb><rt>pun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立起</rb><rt>lih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>喫𢞕</rb><rt>kyʽih-hoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上來</rb><rt>zông-læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>乘勒</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>仇敵</rb><rt>dziu-dih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>震動</rb><rt>cing-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt><rb>十股之一</rb><rt>jih-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt><rb>七千</rb><rt>tsʽih-tsʽin</rt><rb>有名個</rb><rt>yiu-ming-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>yü-to</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>驚駭</rb><rt>kying-hyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>天上個</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽôh-wô*, nying-go ming-z. 或話，人個名字。（原註）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>di-sæn</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>快快</rb><rt>kwʽa-kwʽa</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>第七個</rb><rt>Di-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt><rb>號筒</rb><rt>ʽao-dong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>世間上個</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世</rb><rt>shü-shü</rt><rb>代代</rb><rt>dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>廿四個</rb><rt>nyiæn-s-go</rt><rb>長老</rb><rt>tsiang-lao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> 「<ruby><rb>全能</rb><rt>Djün-neng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來東個</rb><rt>*læ-tong-go*</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>感謝</rb><rt>ken-zia</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>別國</rb><rt>Bih-koh</rt><rb>發怒</rb><rt>fah-nu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>賞賜</rb><rt>sông-s</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>大小</rb><rt>do-siao</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>除滅</rb><rt>djü-mih</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>壞掉</rb><rt>wæ-diao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>開開</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>顯出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>約箱</rb><rt>iah-siang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>殿裏</rb><rt>din-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>響雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mz. XII.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt><rb>現出</rb><rt>yin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>日頭</rb><rt>nyih-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>月亮</rb><rt>yüih-liang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>十二粒</rb><rt>jih-nyi-lih</rt><rb>星</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>身</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>生了</rb><rt>sang-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt><rb>懷懷</rb><rt>wæ-wæ</rt><rb>嘶</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兆頭現出</rb><rt>ziao-deo-yin-cʽih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>紅紅個</rb><rt>ʽong-ʽong-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>七頂</rb><rt>tsʽih-ting</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尾巴</rb><rt>mi-pô</rt><rb>拉勒</rb><rt>l̆a-leh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>天上個</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>星宿</rb><rt>sing-siu</rt><rb>三股之一</rb><rt>sæn-kwu-ts-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>丟來</rb><rt>tiu-læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>龍</rb><rt>Long</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>快生個</rb><rt>kwʽa-sang-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>生落</rb><rt>sang-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吞喫</rb><rt>tʽeng-kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奶花</rb><rt>na-hwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>將來要</rb><rt>tsiang-læ-iao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鐵棍</rb><rt>tʽih-kweng</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>帶上</rb><rt>ta-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文錯印爲 la-leh</sup>

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>逃到</rb><rt>dao-tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>備好個</rb><rt>be-hao-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>二百</rb><rt>nyi-pah</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>米迦勒</rb><rt>Mi-kyüô-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>帶領</rb><rt>ta-ling</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>打弗贏</rb><rt>tang-feh-ying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸處</rb><rt>m-cʽü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>引誘</rb><rt>ying-yiu</rt><rb>咸天下個</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>使差</rb><rt>s-tsʽa</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 原文錯印爲 s-tʽsa</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>成就了</rb><rt>dzing-dziu-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>誣告</rb><rt>vu-kao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>誣告</rb><rt>vu-kao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>趕出了</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>靠</rb><rt>kʽao</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>對證個</rb><rt>te-tsing-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗貪生</rb><rt>feh-tʽen-seng</rt><rb>拚死命個</rb><rt>pʽun-si-ming-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>Sô-yi</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>禍祟</rb><rt>ʽo-se</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>曉得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>嘸多少了</rb><rt>m-to-siao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>龍</rb><rt>Long</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>趕出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>逼難</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>老鷹個</rb><rt>lao-ing-go</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>翼梢</rb><rt>yih-sao</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>飛到</rb><rt>fi-tao</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>養</rb><rt>iang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一年</rb><rt>ih-nyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兩年</rb><rt>liang-nyin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>半年</rb><rt>pun-nyin</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>躲避</rb><rt>to-bi</rt><rb>蛇個</rb><rt>dzô-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽôh-wô*, ih-kyi, liang-kyi, teng pun-kyi. 或話，一季，兩季，等半季。（原註）</sup>

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>蛇</rb><rt>Dzô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>吐出</rb><rt>tʽu-cʽih</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一埭</rb><rt>ih-da</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>吞其掉</rb><rt>tʽeng-gyi-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>Di</rt><rb>幫助</rb><rt>pông-dzu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>吞</rb><rt>tʽeng</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吐出個</rb><rt>tʽu-cʽih-go</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>龍</rb><rt>Long</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>餘多個</rb><rt>yü-to-go</rt><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>誡</rb><rt>kyiæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mz. XIII.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>海邊</rb><rt>hæ-pin</rt><rb>沙灘裏</rb><rt>sô-tʽæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>豹</rb><rt>pao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳</rb><rt>kyiah</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>熊</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>獅子個</rb><rt>s-ts-go</rt><rb>口</rb><rt>kʽeo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打傷</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>快死</rb><rt>kwʽa-si</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>快死個</rb><rt>kwʽa-si-go</rt><rb>傷痕</rb><rt>sông-ʽeng</rt><rb>醫好</rb><rt>i-hao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽEn-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt><rb>跟從</rb><rt>keng-dzong</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>誰</rb><rt>Jü</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>大話</rb><rt>do-wô</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>四十二個</rb><rt>s-jih-nyi-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>開口</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>庉來</rb><rt>deng-læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>管住</rb><rt>kwun-djü</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>庉來</rb><rt>Deng-læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>冊子裏</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>登</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>聽</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>擄掠個</rb><rt>lo-liah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>擄掠</rb><rt>lo-liah</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>殺人個</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>Sing-du-go</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一隻</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>兩隻</rb><rt>liang-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>第一隻</rb><rt>di-ih-tsah</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>打傷</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>快死</rb><rt>kwʽa-si</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>醫好個</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>致使得</rb><rt>cü-s-teh</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>託</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>該</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>傷害</rb><rt>sông-ʽæ</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>活東個</rb><rt>weh-tong-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>靈氣</rb><rt>ling-kyʽi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>講</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>殺掉</rb><rt>sah-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>小</rb><rt>siao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>富貴</rb><rt>fu-kwe</rt><rb>貧賤</rb><rt>bing-zin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>弗做</rb><rt>feh-tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>順隻手</rb><rt>jing-tsah-siu</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>額角頭</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-deo</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一顆</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>記認</rb><rt>kyi-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字個</rb><rt>ming-z-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>打印了</rb><rt>tang-ing-liao</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗許</rb><rt>feh-hyü</rt><rb>買進</rb><rt>ma-tsing</rt><rb>賣出</rb><rt>ma-cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>Dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聰明</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>知識</rb><rt>cü-sih</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>六百</rb><rt>loh-pah</rt><rb>六十</rb><rt>loh-jih</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. XIV.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>郇</u>山</rb><rt>Shing-sæn</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十四</rb><rt>jih-s</rt><rb>萬</rb><rt>væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>額角上</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-zông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿爹個</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>雷個</rb><rt>le-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>彈琴個</rb><rt>dæn-gying-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>彈琴</rb><rt>dæn-gying</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>聲音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位個</rb><rt>zo-we-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>長老個</rb><rt>tsiang-lao-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>簇新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt><rb>讚美詩</rb><rt>tsæn-me-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>取贖個</rb><rt>cʽü-joh-go</rt><rb>十四萬</rb><rt>jih-s-væn</rt><rb>四千</rb><rt>s-tsʽin</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>學</rb><rt>ʽôh</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>讚美詩</rb><rt>tsæn-me-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>女色</rb><rt>nyü-*seh*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>玷污</rb><rt>tin-u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>清潔個</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何裏</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>取贖</rb><rt>cʽü-joh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>嘗新</rb><rt>zông-sing</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>謊話</rb><rt>hwông-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>嘸瑕點個</rb><rt>m-yüô-tin-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>飛來</rb><rt>fi-læ</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永遠個</rb><rt>üong-yün-go</rt><rb>福音</rb><rt>foh-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>傳撥</rb><rt>djün-peh</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>族份</rb><rt>dzoh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>該</rb><rt>Kæ</rt><rb>怕懼</rb><rt>pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>審判個</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>造</rb><rt>zao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>Tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>淫亂</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>觸怒個</rb><rt>cʽoh-nu-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>第三個</rb><rt>di-sæn-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>後背</rb><rt>ʽeo-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>Jü-ko</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>額角上</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-zông</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>必要</rb><rt>pih-iao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>𢛟惶個</rb><rt>ô-wông-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>嘸告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥個</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>夾雜</rb><rt>kah-dzeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>杯子裏</rb><rt>pe-ts-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>受苦</rb><rt>ziu-kwʽu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>痛苦個</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>燻上</rb><rt>hyüing-zông</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗歇</rb><rt>feh-hyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>打印個</rb><rt>tang-ing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>弗得</rb><rt>feh-teh</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>保守</rb><rt>Pao-siu</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>忍耐</rb><rt>jing-næ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>從今以後</rb><rt>dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>屬於</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>安息</rb><rt>en-sih</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生活</rb><rt>sang-weh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一朵</rb><rt>ih-tô</rt><rb>白雲</rb><rt>bah-yüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>雲頂</rb><rt>yüing-teng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>ih-we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>兒子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>坐的</rb><rt>zo-tih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>頭上</rb><rt>deo-zông</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>鋒快個</rb><rt>fong-kwʽa-go</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>收割個</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>百穀</rb><rt>pah-koh</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>熟了</rb><rt>joh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>Zo</rt><rb>雲</rb><rt>yüing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>天上個</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>鋒快個</rb><rt>fong-kwʽa-go</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>祭壇</rb><rt>tsi-dæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>火個</rb><rt>ho-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>捏</rb><rt>nyiah</rt><rb>鋒快</rb><rt>fong-kwʽa</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>鋒快個</rb><rt>fong-kwʽa-go</rt><rb>鐮刀</rb><rt>lin-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收進</rb><rt>siu-tsing</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>葡萄樹個</rb><rt>bu-dao-jü-go</rt><rb>球</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>葡萄果</rb><rt>bu-dao-ko</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>熟了</rb><rt>joh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> ### Mz. XV.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兆頭</rb><rt>ziao-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>從此</rb><rt>dzong-tsʽ</rt><rb>完了</rb><rt>wun-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>玻璃</rb><rt>po-li</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>和攏的個</rb><rt>ʽo-long-tih-go</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>打贏</rb><rt>tang-ying</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立來</rb><rt>lih-læ</rt><rb>玻璃</rb><rt>po-li</rt><rb>海上</rb><rt>hæ-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>琴</rb><rt>gying</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>歌唱</rb><rt>ko-tsʽông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>個</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>讚美詩</rb><rt>tsæn-me-s</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>讚美詩</rb><rt>tsæn-me-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>全能個</rb><rt>Djün-neng-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>作用</rb><rt>tsôh-yüong</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>作爲</rb><rt>tsôh-we</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>誠實</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>誰</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗怕懼</rb><rt>feh-pʽô-gyü</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>讚美</rb><rt>tsæn-me</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>萬國</rb><rt>Væn-koh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>拜服</rb><rt>pa-voh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>顯出了</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一看</rb><rt>ih-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>帳篷個</rb><rt>tsiang-bong-go</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>Sing-din</rt><rb>開間</rb><rt>kʽæ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>掌管</rb><rt>Công-kwun</rt><rb>七樣</rb><rt>tsʽih-yiang</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>清潔</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyih</rt><rb>雪白個</rb><rt>shih-bah-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>穿的</rb><rt>cʽün-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>胸脘頭</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>繫勒</rb><rt>kyi-leh</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>帶</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>四個</rb><rt>s-go</rt><rb>生物</rb><rt>seng-veh</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>永活</rb><rt>üong-weh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>聖殿</rb><rt>Sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt><rb>充滿</rb><rt>cʽong-mun</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇七個</rb><rt>keh-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>七樣</rb><rt>tsʽih-yiang</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>講完</rb><rt>*kông*-wun</rt></ruby>。

---

### Mz. XVI.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>聖殿裏</rb><rt>Sing-din-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟裏</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>第一個</rb><rt>Di-ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>惡毒個</rb><rt>ôh-doh-go</rt><rb>瘡</rb><rt>tsʽông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>第二個</rb><rt>Di-nyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>變做</rb><rt>pin-tso</rt><rb>死人個</rb><rt>si-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>海裏</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>活動個</rb><rt>weh-dong-go</rt><rb>東西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>第三個</rb><rt>Di-sæn-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>江河</rb><rt>kông-ʽo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水源裏</rb><rt>shü-nyün-li</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>變</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>掌管</rb><rt>công-kwun</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>*læ-tong*</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>、<ruby><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>正直個</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup><sup> </sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>聖徒</rb><rt>sing-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>應該個</rb><rt>ing-kæ-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>祭壇裏</rb><rt>tsi-dæn-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>別個</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是個</rb><rt>Z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>爾個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>公義個</rb><rt>kong-yi-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽôh-wô*, ʽeo-deo *læ-tong*-go. 或話，後頭來東個。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] 原書此處缺編號 6</sup>

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>第四個</rb><rt>Di-s-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>日頭裏</rb><rt>nyih-deo-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤎨</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>𤎨勒</rb><rt>pih-leh</rt><rb>熱煞</rb><rt>nyih-sah</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>名頭</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>賜</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>降</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽôh-wô*, me-sah. 或話，𤈦煞。（原註）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽôh-wô*, công-kwun keh-sing tsæ-næn-go gyün-ping. 或話，掌管箇星災難個權柄。（原註）</sup>

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>第五個</rb><rt>Di-ng-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>座位上</rb><rt>zo-we-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>黑暗</rb><rt>heh-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>苦弗過</rb><rt>kwʽu-feh-ko</rt><rb>咬</rb><rt>ngao</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>舌頭</rb><rt>zih-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt><rb>瘡毒</rb><rt>tsʽông-doh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>謗毀</rb><rt>pông-hwe</rt><rb>天上個</rb><rt>tʽin-zông-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>悔改</rb><rt>hwe-kæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>第六個</rb><rt>Di-loh-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯拉</rb><rt>Pah-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大河</rb><rt>do-ʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>燥了</rb><rt>sao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>東邊</rb><rt>tong-pin</rt><rb>君王個</rb><rt>kyüing-wông-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>三個</rb><rt>sæn-go</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>青蛙</rb><rt>tsʽing-w̆ô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>龍個</rb><rt>long-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>口裏</rb><rt>kʽeo-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>惡鬼個</rb><rt>ôh-kyü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>能夠</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>咸天下</rb><rt>ʽen-tʽin-ʽô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>全能</rb><rt>djün-neng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>日子個</rb><rt>nyih-ts-go</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> 「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>賊</rb><rt>zeh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>警醒</rb><rt>kying-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看守</rb><rt>kʽen-siu</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>出膊</rb><rt>cʽih-poh</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>惶恐</rb><rt>wông-kʽong</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯來</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亞馬額頓</rb><rt>Üô-mô-ngah-teng</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>第七個</rb><rt>Di-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>聖殿個</rb><rt>sing-din-go</rt><rb>座位裏</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>完了</rb><rt>Wun-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>響雷</rb><rt>hyiang-le</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>閃電</rb><rt>sin-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>在世</rb><rt>dzæ-shü</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>嘸有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>箇種介</rb><rt>keh-cong-ka</rt><rb>大個</rb><rt>do-go</rt><rb>地震</rb><rt>di-cing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>分開</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt><rb>三橛</rb><rt>sæn-gyüih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>列國個</rb><rt>lih-koh-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>倒塌</rb><rt>tao-tʽah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>Do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>頂</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>𢛟惶個</rb><rt>ô-wông-go</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>一切個</rb><rt>Ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>海島</rb><rt>hæ-tao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>山嶺</rb><rt>sæn-ling</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>看弗見</rb><rt>kʽen-feh-kyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>Ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大約</rb><rt>da-iah</rt><rb>一鈞</rb><rt>ih-kyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>重</rb><rt>djong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>雹子</rb><rt>boh-ts</rt><rb>打傷</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>褻瀆</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>打傷</rb><rt>tang-sông</rt><rb>厲害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 dô</sup>
