### Mz. XVII.

<ruby><rb>捏</rb><rt>Nyiah</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>坐</rb><rt>zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>面頂</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>淫婦</rb><rt>ying-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>庉的個</rb><rt>deng-tih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>喫醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>淫亂個</rb><rt>ying-lön-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>。」<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*sing*-ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>曠野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>紅個</rb><rt>ʽong-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>滿身</rb><rt>mun-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>褻瀆個</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇個</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>紫</rb><rt>ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>紅</rb><rt>ʽong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>寶石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt><rb>裝飾</rb><rt>tsông-sih</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>金子</rb><rt>kying-ts</rt><rb>酒杯</rb><rt>tsiu-pe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>可惡個</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>淫亂個</rb><rt>ying-lön-go</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>額角裏</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>Ao-miao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>淫婦</rb><rt>ying-vu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>可惡個</rb><rt>kʽo-u-go</rt><rb>阿孃</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>喫醉</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tse</rt><rb>聖徒個</rb><rt>sing-du-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大樣</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋會</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>背</rb><rt>pe</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>奧妙</rb><rt>ao-miao</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話向</rb><rt>wô-hyiang</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>嘸底坑裏</rb><rt>m̆-ti-kʽang-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出來</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸於</rb><rt>kwe-ü</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>開闢</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以來</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>冊子裏</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>有個</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>在於</rb><rt>Dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聰明個</rb><rt>tsʽong-ming-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>坐個</rb><rt>zo-go</rt><rb>七座</rb><rt>tsʽih-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>五個</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>敗掉</rb><rt>ba-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>現在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>嘸得個</rb><rt>m-neh-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>第八個</rb><rt>di-pah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在於</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>之內</rb><rt>ts-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>終於</rb><rt>cong-ü</rt><rb>歸到</rb><rt>kwe-tao</rt><rb>滅亡</rb><rt>mih-vông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十個</rb><rt>jih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>還</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>同</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>皇帝個</rb><rt>wông-ti-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>只勒</rb><rt>tsih-leh</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>同心合意</rb><rt>dong-sing-ʽeh-i</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>交戰</rb><rt>kao-tsin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>得勝</rb><rt>teh-sing</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>萬主之</rb><rt>væn-cü-ts</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>萬王之</rb><rt>væn-wông-ts</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>Wông</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>召來</rb><rt>dziao-læ</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>揀選</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>忠信個</rb><rt>cong-sing-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>淫婦</rb><rt>ying-vu</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>坐個</rb><rt>zo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>國份</rb><rt>koh-veng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>十隻</rb><rt>jih-tsah</rt><rb>角</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>淫婦</rb><rt>ying-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>窮苦</rb><rt>gyüong-kwʽu</rt><rb>出膊個</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一心一意</rb><rt>ih-sing-ih-i</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自個</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>國家</rb><rt>koh-kô</rt><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看見個</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>該管</rb><rt>kæ-kwun</rt><rb>地上</rb><rt>di-zông</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽôh-wô*, ken-dong. 或話，感動。（原註）</sup>

---

### Mz. XVIII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>光明</rb><rt>kwông-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳出來</rb><rt>eo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>Tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>跌倒了</rb><rt>tih-tao-de</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>做了</rb><rt>tso-leh</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>庉個</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>邪靈個</rb><rt>zia-ling-go</rt><rb>巢穴</rb><rt>dzao-yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt><rb>嘔憎</rb><rt>eo-tseng</rt><rb>鳥個</rb><rt>tiao-go</rt><rb>巢穴</rb><rt>dzao-yüih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>萬國</rb><rt>væn-koh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>淫亂</rb><rt>ying-lön</rt><rb>觸怒個</rb><rt>cʽoh-nu-go</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>di-zông-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>奢華</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>極頭</rb><rt>gyih-deo</rt><rb>發財了</rb><rt>fah-dzæ-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我個</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>離開</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt></ruby>；<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>罪惡</rb><rt>ze-ôh</rt><rb>滔</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>記得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>壞處</rb><rt>w̆a-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>待</rb><rt>dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>杯子裏</rb><rt>pe-ts-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>加倍</rb><rt>kô-be</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>尊重</rb><rt>tseng-djong</rt><rb>奢華</rb><rt>shæ-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>照數</rb><rt>tsiao-su</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心裏</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>難間</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王后</rb><rt>wông-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>寡婦</rb><rt>kwô-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt><rb>悲傷</rb><rt>pe-shông</rt></ruby>。』<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>一日</rb><rt>ih-nyih</rt><rb>裏向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>橫死</rb><rt>wang-si</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>痛傷</rb><rt>tʽong-sông</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>饑荒</rb><rt>kyi-hwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>𤈦煞</rb><rt>me-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>能幹</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>9, 10</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>君王</rb><rt>kyüing-wông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>奢華個</rb><rt>shæ-wô-go</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>困苦</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kwʽu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>遠遠</rb><rt>yün-yün</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>火着個</rb><rt>ho-dziah-go</rt><rb>煙</rb><rt>in</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>Kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可惜</rb><rt>kʽo-sih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴比倫</rb><rt>Pô-pi-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>堅固個</rb><rt>kyin-kwu-go</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>一時裏</rb><rt>ih-z-li</rt><rb>爾個</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>審判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] 此處原書錯印爲 shæ-wô-gô</sup>

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>地上個</rb><rt>Di-zông-go</rt><rb>客商</rb><rt>kʽah-shông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>哀痛</rb><rt>æ-tʽong</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>買</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>貨物</rb><rt>ho-veh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>金</rb><rt>kying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銀</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>寶石</rb><rt>pao-zah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>珠子</rb><rt>cü-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>紫紅</rb><rt>ts-ʽong</rt><rb>絲線</rb><rt>s-sin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>百樣</rb><rt>pah-yiang</rt><rb>香木</rb><rt>hyiang-moh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>象牙</rb><rt>ziang-ngô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>貴重</rb><rt>kwe-djong</rt><rb>木頭</rb><rt>moh-deo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>銅</rb><rt>dong</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>鐵</rb><rt>tʽih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>玉石個</rb><rt>nyüoh-zah-go</rt><rb>器皿</rb><rt>kyʽi-ming</rt></ruby>；<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>肉桂</rb><rt>nyüoh-kwe</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>香油</rb><rt>hyiang-yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>乳香</rb><rt>jü-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>油</rb><rt>yiu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>細</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>麪粉</rb><rt>min-feng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>麥</rb><rt>mah</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>車子</rb><rt>tsʽô-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>肌身</rb><rt>kyi-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>活靈</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <sup>15</sup> <sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>使徒</rb><rt>s-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>伸冤了</rb><rt>sing-ün-de</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>水個</rb><rt>shü-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>雷個</rb><rt>le-go</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>哈哩路耶</rb><rt>Hah-li-lu-yia</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>全能個</rb><rt>djün-neng-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>執掌</rb><rt>tsih-công</rt><rb>皇帝個權柄</rb><rt>wông-ti-go-gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>快樂</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>歸</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>新婦</rb><rt>Sing-vu</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>備好了</rb><rt>be-hao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>被</rb><rt>Be</rt><rb>請來</rb><rt>tsʽing-læ</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>真話</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>使弗得</rb><rt>S-feh-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>同事個</rb><rt>dong-z-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先知個</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>靈</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>火光</rb><rt>ho-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>頭</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>許多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>冠冕</rb><rt>kwun-min</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>除</rb><rt>djü</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>嘸人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>識得個</rb><rt>sih-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>染血個</rb><rt>nyin-hyüih-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt></ruby>」。<sup>14</sup> <sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>太陽裏</rb><rt>tʽa-yiang-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>謳</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>天空裏</rb><rt>tʽin-kʽong-li</rt><rb>飛個</rb><rt>fi-go</rt><rb>百鳥</rb><rt>pah-tiao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>聚攏</rb><rt>jü-long</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>酒筵</rb><rt>tsiu-yin</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>君王個</rb><rt>kyüing-wông-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>將軍個</rb><rt>tsiang-kyüing-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>英雄個</rb><rt>ing-yüong-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>馬</rb><rt>mô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>騎馬</rb><rt>gyi-mô</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>衆人個</rb><rt>cong-nying-go</rt><rb>肉</rb><rt>nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗論</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>大大小小</rb><rt>do-do-siao-siao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>抲去</rb><rt>kʽô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>同隊</rb><rt>dong-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>迷惑</rb><rt>mi-ʽôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>野獸個</rb><rt>yia-siu-go</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>兩</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活個</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>來的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>着個</rb><rt>dziah-go</rt><rb>火坑裏</rb><rt>ho-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> ### Mz. XX.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>嘸底坑個</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang-go</rt><rb>鑰匙</rb><rt>yiah-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>鏈條</rb><rt>lin-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>箇個</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>龍</rb><rt>long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>老蛇</rb><rt>lao-dzô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>Mo-kwe</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>縛</rb><rt>bo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>丟</rb><rt>tiu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>嘸底坑裏</rb><rt>m-ti-kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>上頭</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>封皮</rb><rt>fong-bi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>哄誘</rb><rt>hong-yiu</rt><rb>列國</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>後頭</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>暫時</rb><rt>dzæn-z</rt><rb>釋放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>好幾個</rb><rt>hao-kyi-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>審判個</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>權柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>爲了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶穌</u>個</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>斬頭</rb><rt>tsæn-deo</rt><rb>主顧個</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>靈魂</rb><rt>ling-weng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>額角裏</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-li</rt><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>手裏</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>活東</rb><rt>weh-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>餘多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>Keh-z</rt><rb>第一遭</rb><rt>di-ih-tsao</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>第一</rb><rt>di-ih</rt><rb>活轉來</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>聖善個</rb><rt>sing-jün-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>弗能夠</rb><rt>feh-neng-keo</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>一千</rb><rt>ih-tsʽin</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>滿了</rb><rt>mun-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>撒但</rb><rt>Sah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>放出</rb><rt>fông-cʽih</rt><rb>牢監</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>，<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt><rb>哄誘</rb><rt>hong-yiu</rt><rb>天四邊個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-pin-go</rt><rb>列國</rb><rt>lih-koh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>谷</rb><rt>Kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>瑪各</rb><rt>Mô-kôh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>聚</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>攏來</rb><rt>long-læ</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>數目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>海裏個</rb><rt>hæ-li-go</rt><rb>沙泥</rb><rt>sô-nyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>哄誘</rb><rt>Hong-yiu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>丟落</rb><rt>tiu-lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>硫磺</rb><rt>liu-wông</rt><rb>坑裏</rb><rt>kʽang-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>野獸</rb><rt>yia-siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>丟落間個</rb><rt>tiu-lôh-kæn-go</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>日夜</rb><rt>nyih-yia</rt><rb>痛苦</rb><rt>tʽong-kwʽu</rt><rb>永遠</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗歇</rb><rt>feh-hyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>雪白</rb><rt>shih-bah</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>坐的個</rb><rt>zo-tih-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>避過</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>並且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>嘸處</rb><rt>m-cʽü</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>死過了</rb><rt>si-ko-liao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>小小</rb><rt>siao-siao</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>攤開</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一本</rb><rt>ih-peng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>攤開</rb><rt>tʽæn-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>冊子裏</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>Hæ</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>交出</rb><rt>kao-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>墳墓</rb><rt>veng-mo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>交出</rb><rt>kao-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>裏頭</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>死個</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>判斷</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> ### Mz. XXI.

<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>簇新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>簇新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>過了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>簇新個</rb><rt>tsʽoh-sing-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>預先</rb><rt>yü-sin</rt><rb>備辦</rb><rt>be-bæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>裝飾</rb><rt>tsông-sih</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>男𡢿</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>聲響</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明個</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>帳篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>來東</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其拉個</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>揩燥</rb><rt>kʽa-sao</rt><rb>一切個</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>眼淚</rb><rt>ngæn-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>傷心</rb><rt>sông-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>啼哭</rb><rt>di-kʽoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>勞苦</rb><rt>lao-kwʽu</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>前頭</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>過去了</rb><rt>ko-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>坐</rb><rt>Zo</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>新造</rb><rt>sing-zao</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>完了</rb><rt>Wun-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亞勒巴</rb><rt>Üô-leh-pô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿米加</rb><rt>O-mi-kyüô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>賜撥</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>口渴個</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>七個</rb><rt>tsʽih-go</rt><rb>香爐</rb><rt>hyiang-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>𣥼滿</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>七樣</rb><rt>tsʽih-yiang</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其中</rb><rt>gyi-cong</rt><rb>一個</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>走攏</rb><rt>tseo-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>老𡢿</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>蒙</rb><rt>mong</rt><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*sing*-ling</rt><rb>感動</rb><rt>ken-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>領</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一座</rb><rt>ih-zo</rt><rb>高大個</rb><rt>kao-do-go</rt><rb>山頂</rb><rt>sæn-teng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>烏碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>降落來</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>城牆</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二道</rb><rt>jih-nyi-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>十二個</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>門上</rb><rt>meng-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>寫的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>子孫</rb><rt>ts-seng</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>支派個</rb><rt>ts-pʽa-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>東邊</rb><rt>Tong-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>北邊</rb><rt>poh-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>南邊</rb><rt>nen-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>西邊</rb><rt>si-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>門</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>Teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>講個</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>捏勒</rb><rt>nyiah-leh</rt><rb>金子個</rb><rt>kying-ts-go</rt><rb>丈尺</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>城牆</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>城子</rb><rt>Dzing-ts</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>轉方</rb><rt>cün-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>長</rb><rt>dziang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt><rb>一色斯樣</rb><rt>ih-seh-s-yiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>丈尺</rb><rt>dziang-tsʽih</rt><rb>量量</rb><rt>liang-liang</rt><rb>城子</rb><rt>dzing-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>里</rb><rt>li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>長</rb><rt>Dziang</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>闊</rb><rt>kwʽeh</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>高</rb><rt>kao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>一樣個</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>量</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>城牆</rb><rt>dzing-ziang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>四十四</rb><rt>s-jih-s</rt><rb>尺</rb><rt>tsʽah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>人個</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>量度</rb><rt>liang-doh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>天使個</rb><rt>tʽin-s-go</rt><rb>量度</rb><rt>liang-doh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>第五</rb><rt>di-ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>雜紅玉</rb><rt>dzeh-ʽong-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第六</rb><rt>di-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瑪瑙</rb><rt>mô-nao</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第七</rb><rt>di-tsʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黃玉</rb><rt>wông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第八</rb><rt>di-pah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>綠玉</rb><rt>loh-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第九</rb><rt>di-kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>淡黃玉</rb><rt>dæn-wông-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>第十</rb><rt>di-jih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翡翠</rb><rt>fi-tsʽe</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十一</rb><rt>jih-ih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>赤玉</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyüoh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>紫玉</rb><rt>ts-nyüoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>城門</rb><rt>dzing-meng</rt><rb>整日</rb><rt>tsing-nyih</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>關</rb><rt>kwæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>列國個</rb><rt>Lih-koh-go</rt><rb>榮華</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尊貴</rb><rt>tseng-kwe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>帶進來</rb><rt>ta-tsing-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>潔淨</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>可惡</rb><rt>kʽo-u</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走弗進</rb><rt>tseo-feh-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>寫</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>冊子裏</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts-li</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>走進</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt></ruby>。

### Mz. XXII.

<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>箇埭</rb><rt>keh-da</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>水晶</rb><rt>se-tsing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>梗青個</rb><rt>kwang-tsʽing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>流出來</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大街</rb><rt>do-ka</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>河個</rb><rt>ʽo-go</rt><rb>兩邊</rb><rt>liang-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>十二種</rb><rt>jih-nyi-cong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每</rb><rt>me</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt><rb>結</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>其自個</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>樹個</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>葉</rb><rt>yih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>醫</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>國份個</rb><rt>koh-veng-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>以後</rb><rt>Yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>咒罵</rb><rt>tsiu-mô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>小羊個</rb><rt>Siao-yiang-go</rt><rb>座位</rb><rt>zo-we</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>服侍</rb><rt>voh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>；<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>面孔</rb><rt>min-kʽong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>額角上</rb><rt>ngah-kôh-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇頭</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>嘸得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>用着</rb><rt>yüong-djôh</rt><rb>燈盞</rb><rt>teng-tsæn</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>日頭個</rb><rt>nyih-deo-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>光照</rb><rt>kwông-tsiao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>王</rb><rt>wông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世世代代</rb><rt>shü-shü-dæ-dæ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>忠信</rb><rt>cong-sing</rt><rb>誠實個</rb><rt>dzing-jih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>聖善</rb><rt>Sing-jün</rt><rb>先知人個</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>應驗</rb><rt>ing-nyiæn</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>告訴</rb><rt>kao-su</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>約翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>既然</rb><rt>Kyi-jün</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>看見</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伏倒</rb><rt>boh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>腳下</rb><rt>kyiah-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>拜拜</rb><rt>pa-pa</rt><rb>指點</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>使弗得</rb><rt>S-feh-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>還有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>同事個</rb><rt>dong-z-go</rt><rb>奴僕</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗可</rb><rt>Feh-kʽo</rt><rb>封好</rb><rt>fong-hao</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知個</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>因爲</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>時候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>近了</rb><rt>gying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>不義</rb><rt>Peh-yi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>不義</rb><rt>peh-yi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>污穢</rb><rt>u-w̆e</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>公義</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍舊</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>聖善</rb><rt>sing-jün</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>報應</rb><rt>pao-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>各人個</rb><rt>kôh-nying-go</rt><rb>行爲</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>報</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亞勒巴</rb><rt>Üô-leh-pô</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿米加</rb><rt>O-mi-kyüô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前</rb><rt>zin</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>後</rb><rt>ʽeo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>起頭</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>結煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>14</sup> 「<ruby><rb>守</rb><rt>Siu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>號令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福氣</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>樹</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>可以</rb><rt>kʽo-yi</rt><rb>從</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>門口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>進</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>城</rb><rt>dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>外頭</rb><rt>Nga-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>惡狗</rb><rt>ôh-keo</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>邪術</rb><rt>zia-jih</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>姦淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>兇手</rb><rt>hyüong-siu</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>偶像</rb><rt>ngeo-ziang</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>歡喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>假</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已經</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>衆</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>教會</rb><rt>kyiao-we</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大闢</u>個</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>根株</rb><rt>keng-cü</rt></ruby>、<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其個</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>後代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇粒</rb><rt>keh-lih</rt><rb>明亮個</rb><rt>ming-liang-go</rt><rb>五更曉</rb><rt>ng-kang-hyiao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>聖靈</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>新婦</rb><rt>sing-vu</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>聽聞個</rb><rt>Tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>口渴個</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>要個</rb><rt>iao-go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>白白</rb><rt>bah-bah</rt><rb>喫</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>活命個</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>聽聞</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>個</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>對證</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>加上</rb><rt>kô-zông</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>災難</rb><rt>tsæ-næn</rt><rb>撥</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>減落</rb><rt>kæn-lôh</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>先知個</rb><rt>sin-cü-go</rt><rb>說話</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>會</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>減</rb><rt>kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>冊子</rb><rt>tsʽah-ts</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>聖城裏</rb><rt>sing-dzing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>連</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>書裏</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>寫的個</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>好處</rb><rt>hao-cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>許</rb><rt>hyü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>有份</rb><rt>yiu-veng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>對證</rb><rt>Te-tsing</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事幹</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顧</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>話</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>實在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>速速</rb><rt>soh-soh</rt><rb>來其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>爾</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>來</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但願</rb><rt>Dæn-nyün</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶穌</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>基督</u>個</rb><rt>Kyi-toh-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>臨着</rb><rt>ling-djôh</rt><rb>爾拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>衆人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亞們</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>新約</rb><rt>SING-IAH</rt><rb>全書個</rb><rt>DJÜN-SHÜ-GO</rt><rb>終</rb><rt>CONG</rt></ruby>
